# Cold adversarial review dispatch — G78 owner join

Review only the sealed intake defined by `REVIEW_MANIFEST.tsv`. Do not edit files, continue the
research, inspect anything outside the intake, or access the protected stopped native-on-shell
draft.

## Cold foundation

UDT begins with observed clock/ruler calibration `c_E` and a reciprocal comparison acting on a
supplied ordered depth. In the bounded G75 stationary axial control family the metric is

```text
ds^2 = -A(x)c_E^2 dt^2 + R^2 dx^2/A(x)
       + R^2 x^2(dtheta^2 + sin^2(theta)dpsi^2)
       + 2 R c_E h(x) sin^2(theta) dt dpsi,
A(x)=1+a x^2, h(x)=x^2 q(x^2).
```

G75 contains 591 exact center-smooth Lorentz-regular controls. G76 maps their complete sampled sky
relations under one supplied observer/end-sphere query. G77 replays every ray directly through the
metric Christoffel equation and returns 590 strong, one registered, zero unresolved cross-method
rows. None of these packages claims a physical profile, endpoint, scale, or source.

## Claim to attack

G78 reports:

```text
NO_PHYSICAL_PROFILE_ENDPOINT_SCALE_OR_SOURCE_OWNER_IN_FROZEN_G78_UNIVERSE
```

It claims exactly, within twenty frozen sources, that:

1. the registered regularity/topology gates select none of the 591 profiles;
2. `tau=c_E t/R` factors the metric as `ds^2=R^2 dSigma^2`, so the dimensionless null sky relation
   cannot determine constant `R` while UDT itself is not thereby scale-free;
3. the chosen `x=1` comparison sphere is not identified with `X_max`;
4. SNe P1 is a compatibility anchor with no owned map into G75 `A`, `q`, or endpoint; and
5. an invertible response transports unrestricted positive-definite source covariance but does not
   derive its physical state.

## Required adversarial tasks

1. Reconstruct the exact source scope and detect any missing current source that already owns a
   route.
2. Recheck `591=49*4*3+3`, the G77 `590/1/0` census, and every sampled no-defect assertion.
3. Independently derive the scale factorization, including the Christoffel/null-geodesic consequence.
   State whether `c_E` or `R` re-enters through affine parameter, observer normalization, endpoint
   typing, or an omitted response channel in a way that defeats the scoped conclusion.
4. Attempt the strongest current derivation identifying `x=1`, `R`, or `X_max`. Reject any
   identification based only on notation or analogy.
5. Attempt the strongest current map from SNe P1 `(R_w,n,phi_pair)` to G75 `(R,a,q,x_endpoint)`.
6. Recheck the source-congruence theorem and its domain. Determine whether sampled degree-one
   orientation evidence is enough for the way G78 uses it; prevent promotion to continuum.
7. Audit every route disposition and try to find an owned profile, endpoint/scale, or source law.
8. Inspect the scripts for circular verification, weak catches, post-outcome retuning, or merit-based
   filtering.
9. Judge whether the proposed same-geometry cross-query SNe map is truly the smallest constructive
   next joint, or name a smaller metric-led calculation.

# G78 exact derivation — profile, endpoint/scale, and source-owner join

Date: 2026-08-11

## 1. What is being joined

G77 supplies a verified evaluator for 591 stationary axial control geometries under one supplied
observer and comparison sphere. G78 asks whether any current result supplies the missing physical
owner of the profile, endpoint/global scale, or source state. It does not ask which control looks
most like the CMB.

The frozen source universe has twenty individually adjudicated records. Seven candidate routes are
typed in `OWNER_ROUTE_LEDGER.tsv`; none is silently enlarged after seeing the outcome.

## 2. Regularity and topology do not select inside this family

The exact G75 identity is

```text
591 = 49 primitive quadratic shapes * 4 amplitudes * 3 lapse controls + 3 zero controls.
```

G77 returns `590 STRONG_DIRECT_AGREEMENT`, one `REGISTERED_DIRECT_AGREEMENT`, and zero unresolved
rows. All 591 maps have all 2,562 sampled directions crossing, sampled degree one, and zero missing,
negative-face, negative-projected-map, or registered near-area defects.

Therefore the registered center/signature/finite-mesh relation gates characterize all 591 controls
as survivors. They select zero rows. This is not a continuum injectivity theorem and not a claim
that regularity can never select in a broader family.

## 3. Exact scale factorization

The G75 metric is

```text
ds^2 = -A(x)c_E^2 dt^2 + R^2 dx^2/A(x)
       + R^2 x^2(dtheta^2 + sin^2(theta)dpsi^2)
       + 2 R c_E h(x) sin^2(theta) dt dpsi.
```

Define the dimensionless time coordinate

```text
tau = c_E t/R.
```

Then exactly

```text
ds^2 = R^2[-A dtau^2 + dx^2/A
            + x^2(dtheta^2 + sin^2(theta)dpsi^2)
            + 2 h sin^2(theta) dtau dpsi]
     = R^2 dSigma^2.
```

Because `R` is constant, the Levi-Civita connection of `R^2 dSigma^2` in these dimensionless
coordinates equals that of `dSigma^2`. Hence the unparameterized null paths and their dimensionless
angular relation are independent of the numerical value of `R`. The same substitution uses `c_E`
to define the clock/ruler-compatible time coordinate, after which `c_E` is absent from the
dimensionless angular map.

This does **not** mean UDT is scale-free. `c_E` remains the observed clock/ruler calibration and
`R` remains the conversion from dimensionless `x` to physical length. The result is narrower:

```text
the G75--G77 dimensionless angular relation cannot determine its own absolute R.
```

An external distance/clock observable or a same-geometry cross-query relation is required.

## 4. The control endpoint is not `X_max`

G76/G77 choose the first outward crossing of `x=1`. The choice is part of the observer query. The
current `X_max` frame instead requires a nonnegative observer-pair separation to approach a shared
asymptote only as reciprocal depth diverges. It explicitly supplies no separation law, numerical
value, chart endpoint, or identification with `R`.

Thus `x=1`, `R`, and `X_max` have different types. No frozen source contains a map identifying
them. The asymptotic requirement remains necessary for a future physical realization but is not an
endpoint or scale owner here.

## 5. The SNe anchor has the wrong current input type to select G75

The frozen SNe P1 relation is

```text
r(phi_pair)=R_w[1-exp(-2 phi_pair/n)].
```

It is an observed conditional low-redshift observer-pair relation. G75 instead supplies a lapse
control `A(x)=1+a x^2`, an axial mixing profile `h=x^2 q(x^2)`, and a chosen comparison sphere.
No frozen source maps `(R_w,n,phi_pair)` into `(R,a,q,x_endpoint)` by deriving both from one complete
geometry and query.

P1 may later test a complete geometry for compatibility. Copying it into `A` or `q` would be a
role error. It therefore remains `COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY`.

## 6. Geometry transports source state but does not populate it

For any invertible screen response `D` and positive-definite observed covariance `C_obs`, define

```text
C_src(D)=D^-1 C_obs D^-T.
```

Then exactly

```text
D C_src(D) D^T = C_obs,
```

and `C_src(D)` remains positive definite. G78 verifies the symbolic identity and an independent
256-case nonsymmetric numerical reconstruction. Therefore an unrestricted source can absorb an
invertible geometric response. The response evaluates and transports a source; it does not derive
the source's shape, normalization, or statistics.

G70's known-source-plus-carry model can distinguish controls conditionally. But the known source
and observable carry channel are themselves unowned premises. Conditional identifiability is not
physical ownership.

This exact source statement applies on the invertible-response stratum. G77 supplies strong finite-
mesh evidence that all 591 sampled relation maps are regular and orientation preserving, but G78
does not promote that evidence to a continuum diffeomorphism theorem.

## 7. Landing and smallest remaining joint

The seven-route result is:

```text
4 OPEN_NO_OWNER
1 COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY
1 NECESSARY_REQUIREMENT_ONLY
1 CONDITIONAL_IDENTIFIABILITY_ONLY
0 OWNED_NATIVE
```

Primary bounded landing:

```text
NO_PHYSICAL_PROFILE_ENDPOINT_SCALE_OR_SOURCE_OWNER_IN_FROZEN_G78_UNIVERSE
```

The smallest constructive next joint is not another ray solver or an attractive-profile ranking.
It is one same-complete-geometry realization linking an actual observer query to both a dimensional
distance/clock observable and the angular relation. The most accessible existing compatibility
test is SNe, but its full query must be derived or explicitly supplied before P1 can constrain the
G75 family. A native source-state law is the separate, deeper route. Bootstrap remains a hypothesis
and did not enter G78.

# G78 audit report — profile, endpoint/scale, and source-owner join

Date: 2026-08-11

Status: `PROVISIONAL_INTERNALLY_VERIFIED__FRESH_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED`

## Landing

`NO_PHYSICAL_PROFILE_ENDPOINT_SCALE_OR_SOURCE_OWNER_IN_FROZEN_G78_UNIVERSE`

The completed G75--G77 geometry is a verified conditional evaluator, not a physical selector.
Within the exact twenty-source G78 universe, none of seven preregistered routes owns the physical
profile, comparison endpoint/global scale, or source state.

## What was learned

1. All 591 registered profiles survive the exact center/signature gates and the sampled G77
   whole-sky relation gates. Those gates characterize the family but select no member.
2. With `tau=c_E t/R`, the supplied metric factors exactly as `ds^2=R^2 dSigma^2`. The
   dimensionless null sky relation therefore cannot determine `R`. This is not a return to CSN or
   a claim that UDT is scale-free: `c_E` remains the observed calibration and `R` remains physical.
3. The control sphere `x=1` is not `X_max`. No current realization map joins them.
4. The SNe P1 relation is a useful low-redshift compatibility anchor, but it is not a G75 lapse or
   mixing profile and currently selects neither the CMB profile nor endpoint.
5. An invertible response transports an unrestricted positive-definite source covariance exactly;
   it does not derive source shape, normalization, or statistics. Conditional multichannel
   identifiability still depends on unowned source and observable premises.

## Evidence

- exact source universe: `20/20` hashes at base `9a78af8`;
- frozen profile census: `591 = 588 + 3`, all unique;
- direct G77 census: `590/1/0`, with zero registered sampled orientation defects;
- exact constant-scale factorization: `5/5` coefficients;
- exact symbolic source-congruence identity plus 16 production controls;
- independent source-congruence replay: 256 nonsymmetric controls, maximum relative residual
  `2.1881601280209868e-14`, minimum source eigenvalue `0.012977997910275318`;
- semantic catches: `10/10`.

## Four evidence gates

1. **Preregistered:** yes, commit `bd500a09`, before algebra, census, or route adjudication.
2. **Full or bounded:** complete for the exact twenty-source, seven-route universe and all 591
   frozen profiles; finite-mesh and stationary-axial, not generic complete UDT.
3. **Independently verified:** production and separately written algebra/census implementations
   agree; fresh zero-context adversarial review remains required.
4. **Premises audited:** yes; every scale, endpoint, profile, source, query, and inactive downstream
   premise is recorded in `PREMISE_LEDGER.tsv`.

## Authority boundary

No profile was ranked, fitted, removed, or selected. No physical CMB source, endpoint, `R`,
`X_max`, polarization, TT/TE/EE/BB population, bootstrap rule, action, matter source, or signalling
law was used or inferred. The seven protected stopped-draft paths remained unread and untouched.

## Next gate after external review

If the bounded landing survives review, construct the smallest same-geometry cross-query map:
derive or explicitly type one complete SNe observer query on the G75/G77 geometry, retaining the
full angular/mixing channels, and test its dimensional distance/clock output against the frozen P1
compatibility anchor. Do not copy P1 into the geometry or use it to fit/rank profiles. The native
source-state law remains a separate deeper program.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Derive the bounded G78 profile/endpoint/source ownership join."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import sympy as sp


HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent
BASE = "9a78af889321d84914ae5eb2c066da56bc957719"


def rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        return list(csv.DictReader(stream, delimiter="\t"))


def digest_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def verify_sources() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    manifest = rows(HERE / "SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv")
    assert len(manifest) == 20
    assert len({row["path"] for row in manifest}) == 20
    for row in manifest:
        assert row["source_commit"] == BASE
        data = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{BASE}:{row['path']}"], cwd=ROOT)
        assert digest_bytes(data) == row["sha256"], row["path"]
    return manifest


def exact_scale_factorization() -> dict[str, object]:
    R, c_e, A, h, st = sp.symbols("R c_E A h sin_theta", positive=True, nonzero=True)
    dt_dtau = R / c_e
    original = {
        "tau_tau": -A * c_e**2 * dt_dtau**2,
        "x_x": R**2 / A,
        "theta_theta": R**2,
        "psi_psi_unit_x2": R**2,
        "tau_psi": 2 * R * c_e * h * st**2 * dt_dtau,
    }
    expected = {
        "tau_tau": -A,
        "x_x": 1 / A,
        "theta_theta": 1,
        "psi_psi_unit_x2": 1,
        "tau_psi": 2 * h * st**2,
    }
    checks = {key: sp.simplify(original[key] / R**2 - expected[key]) == 0 for key in original}
    assert all(checks.values())
    return {
        "substitution": "tau=c_E*t/R",
        "factorization": "ds^2=R^2*dSigma^2",
        "coefficient_checks": checks,
        "interpretation": "dimensionless_null_sky_relation_independent_of_constant_R_and_unit_calibration_cE",
        "non_implication": "does_not_make_UDT_scale_free_or_select_R_endpoint_or_Xmax",
    }


def exact_source_congruence() -> dict[str, object]:
    a, b, c, d, u, v, w = sp.symbols("a b c d u v w")
    D = sp.Matrix([[a, b], [c, d]])
    C_obs = sp.Matrix([[u, v], [v, w]])
    C_src = D.inv() * C_obs * D.inv().T
    residual = sp.simplify(D * C_src * D.T - C_obs)
    assert residual == sp.zeros(2)

    max_relative = 0.0
    minimum_source_eigenvalue = float("inf")
    controls = 0
    for k in range(1, 17):
        Dn = np.array([[1.0 + k / 19.0, (-1) ** k / 7.0], [k / 23.0, 0.8 + k / 31.0]])
        if abs(np.linalg.det(Dn)) < 0.1:
            continue
        B = np.array([[1.0 + k / 13.0, k / 29.0], [(-1) ** k / 11.0, 0.7 + k / 17.0]])
        Cn = B @ B.T + 0.2 * np.eye(2)
        source = np.linalg.solve(Dn, Cn) @ np.linalg.inv(Dn).T
        reconstructed = Dn @ source @ Dn.T
        relative = np.linalg.norm(reconstructed - Cn) / np.linalg.norm(Cn)
        max_relative = max(max_relative, float(relative))
        minimum_source_eigenvalue = min(minimum_source_eigenvalue, float(np.linalg.eigvalsh(source).min()))
        controls += 1
    assert controls == 16 and max_relative < 2e-15 and minimum_source_eigenvalue > 0
    return {
        "symbolic_residual_zero": True,
        "deterministic_controls": controls,
        "maximum_relative_residual": max_relative,
        "minimum_source_eigenvalue": minimum_source_eigenvalue,
        "interpretation": "invertible_geometry_transports_arbitrary_positive_definite_source_data_but_does_not_derive_it",
    }


def family_census() -> dict[str, object]:
    profiles = rows(ROOT / "udt_cmb_G75_center_regular_axial_profile_family_2026-08-11/PROFILE_ATLAS.tsv")
    direct = rows(ROOT / "udt_cmb_G77_full_family_direct_christoffel_replay_2026-08-11/DIRECT_CHRISTOFFEL_ATLAS.tsv")
    assert len(profiles) == len(direct) == 591
    assert len({row["profile_id"] for row in profiles}) == 591
    assert {row["profile_id"] for row in profiles} == {row["profile_id"] for row in direct}
    nonzero = [row for row in profiles if row["shape_id"] != "ZERO"]
    zero = [row for row in profiles if row["shape_id"] == "ZERO"]
    assert len(nonzero) == 49 * 4 * 3 == 588 and len(zero) == 3
    classes: dict[str, int] = {}
    for row in direct:
        classes[row["direct_class"]] = classes.get(row["direct_class"], 0) + 1
        assert int(row["crossed_vertices"]) == 2562
        assert int(row["missing_vertices"]) == 0
        assert int(row["negative_faces"]) == 0
        assert int(row["negative_projected_face_maps"]) == 0
        assert int(row["near_area_1e2"]) == 0
        assert abs(float(row["degree"]) - 1.0) < 5e-15
    assert classes == {"STRONG_DIRECT_AGREEMENT": 590, "REGISTERED_DIRECT_AGREEMENT": 1}
    return {
        "profiles": len(profiles),
        "nonzero_profiles": len(nonzero),
        "zero_controls": len(zero),
        "direct_classes": classes,
        "sampled_degree_one": 591,
        "sampled_missing_or_orientation_defects": 0,
        "selector_rows_from_regular_topology": 0,
    }

        {"route": "P_GLOBAL_RELATION", "target": "PHYSICAL_PROFILE", "status": "OPEN_NO_OWNER", "evidence": "all_591_sampled_relations_are_degree_one_orientation_preserving", "blocker": "relation_invariants_evaluate_each_supplied_profile_but_do_not_select_one"},
        {"route": "E_SCALE", "target": "PHYSICAL_ENDPOINT_OR_GLOBAL_SCALE", "status": "OPEN_NO_OWNER", "evidence": "tau_cE_t_over_R_gives_ds2_equals_R2_dSigma2", "blocker": "dimensionless_null_sky_relation_is_blind_to_constant_R_while_cE_remains_unit_calibration"},
        {"route": "E_SNE", "target": "PHYSICAL_ENDPOINT_OR_GLOBAL_SCALE", "status": "COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY", "evidence": "P1_is_r_of_phi_pair_with_observed_low_z_parameters", "blocker": "no_owned_map_from_P1_Rw_n_to_G75_A_q_or_control_endpoint"},

def dependency_edges() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    return [
        {"source": "c_E", "target": "tau_coordinate", "relation": "calibrates_clock_to_ruler", "owner_status": "OBSERVED_FOUNDATIONAL_ANCHOR"},

def write_tsv(path: Path, data: list[dict[str, str]]) -> None:
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=list(data[0]), delimiter="\t", lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(data)

    scale = exact_scale_factorization()
    source = exact_source_congruence()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent reconstruction of the load-bearing G78 claims."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import sympy as sp


HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent
BASE = "9a78af889321d84914ae5eb2c066da56bc957719"


def table(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        return list(csv.DictReader(stream, delimiter="\t"))


def main() -> None:
    manifest = table(HERE / "SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv")
    assert len(manifest) == len({row["path"] for row in manifest}) == 20
    for row in manifest:
        data = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{BASE}:{row['path']}"], cwd=ROOT)
        assert hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() == row["sha256"]

    profiles = table(ROOT / "udt_cmb_G75_center_regular_axial_profile_family_2026-08-11/PROFILE_ATLAS.tsv")
    replay = table(ROOT / "udt_cmb_G77_full_family_direct_christoffel_replay_2026-08-11/DIRECT_CHRISTOFFEL_ATLAS.tsv")
    profile_ids = {row["profile_id"] for row in profiles}
    replay_ids = {row["profile_id"] for row in replay}
    assert len(profiles) == len(profile_ids) == len(replay) == len(replay_ids) == 591
    assert profile_ids == replay_ids
    assert sum(row["shape_id"] == "ZERO" for row in profiles) == 3
    assert sum(row["direct_class"] == "STRONG_DIRECT_AGREEMENT" for row in replay) == 590
    assert sum(row["direct_class"] == "REGISTERED_DIRECT_AGREEMENT" for row in replay) == 1
    assert all(int(row["crossed_vertices"]) == 2562 for row in replay)
    assert all(int(row["missing_vertices"]) == 0 for row in replay)
    assert all(int(row["negative_faces"]) == 0 for row in replay)
    assert all(int(row["negative_projected_face_maps"]) == 0 for row in replay)
    assert all(int(row["near_area_1e2"]) == 0 for row in replay)
    assert max(abs(float(row["degree"]) - 1.0) for row in replay) < 5e-15

    R, c_e, A, h, st = sp.symbols("R c_E A h sin_theta", positive=True)
    dt = R / c_e
    normalized = sp.Matrix([
        -A * c_e**2 * dt**2 / R**2,
        (R**2 / A) / R**2,
        R**2 / R**2,
        (2 * R * c_e * h * st**2 * dt) / R**2,
    ])
    expected = sp.Matrix([-A, 1 / A, 1, 2 * h * st**2])
    assert all(sp.simplify(value) == 0 for value in normalized - expected)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(7801)
    max_relative = 0.0
    min_source_eigenvalue = float("inf")
    for _ in range(256):
        D = rng.normal(size=(2, 2))
        while abs(np.linalg.det(D)) < 0.15:
            D = rng.normal(size=(2, 2))
        B = rng.normal(size=(2, 2))
        C_obs = B @ B.T + 0.1 * np.eye(2)
        D_inv = np.linalg.inv(D)
        C_src = D_inv @ C_obs @ D_inv.T
        reconstructed = D @ C_src @ D.T
        max_relative = max(max_relative, float(np.linalg.norm(reconstructed - C_obs) / np.linalg.norm(C_obs)))
        min_source_eigenvalue = min(min_source_eigenvalue, float(np.linalg.eigvalsh(C_src).min()))
    assert max_relative < 2e-13 and min_source_eigenvalue > 0

    routes = table(HERE / "OWNER_ROUTE_LEDGER.tsv")
    assert [row["route"] for row in routes] == [
        "P_REGULARITY", "P_GLOBAL_RELATION", "E_SCALE", "E_SNE", "E_XMAX",
        "S_GEOMETRY", "S_MULTICHANNEL",
    ]
    expected_status = {
        "P_REGULARITY": "OPEN_NO_OWNER",
        "P_GLOBAL_RELATION": "OPEN_NO_OWNER",
        "E_SCALE": "OPEN_NO_OWNER",
        "E_SNE": "COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY",
        "E_XMAX": "NECESSARY_REQUIREMENT_ONLY",
        "S_GEOMETRY": "OPEN_NO_OWNER",
        "S_MULTICHANNEL": "CONDITIONAL_IDENTIFIABILITY_ONLY",
    }
    assert {row["route"]: row["status"] for row in routes} == expected_status
    assert all(row["evidence"] and row["blocker"] for row in routes)

    result = json.loads((HERE / "DERIVATION_RESULT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert result["landing"] == "NO_PHYSICAL_PROFILE_ENDPOINT_SCALE_OR_SOURCE_OWNER_IN_FROZEN_G78_UNIVERSE"
    assert result["owned_native_routes"] == 0
    assert result["scale_factorization"]["non_implication"] == "does_not_make_UDT_scale_free_or_select_R_endpoint_or_Xmax"

    output = {
        "schema": "udt-cmb-g78-independent-verification-v1",
        "status": "PASS",
        "source_rows": len(manifest),
        "profile_rows": len(profiles),
        "replay_rows": len(replay),
        "scale_factorization_reproduced": True,
        "source_congruence_controls": 256,
        "source_congruence_maximum_relative_residual": max_relative,
        "source_congruence_minimum_source_eigenvalue": min_source_eigenvalue,
        "route_rows": len(routes),
        "owned_native_routes": 0,
    }
    (HERE / "INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION.json").write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(output, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exercise fail-closed semantic catches for the G78 result layer."""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path


HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def table(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        return list(csv.DictReader(stream, delimiter="\t"))


def validate(result: dict, routes: list[dict[str, str]]) -> None:
    assert result["family"]["profiles"] == 591
    assert result["family"]["sampled_degree_one"] == 591
    assert result["family"]["selector_rows_from_regular_topology"] == 0
    assert result["maximum_scope"] == "exact_twenty_source_ownership_audit_plus_frozen_591_profile_finite_mesh_census"
    assert result["scale_factorization"]["non_implication"] == "does_not_make_UDT_scale_free_or_select_R_endpoint_or_Xmax"
    assert result["owned_native_routes"] == 0
    by_route = {row["route"]: row for row in routes}
    assert len(by_route) == len(routes) == 7
    assert by_route["P_REGULARITY"]["status"] == "OPEN_NO_OWNER"
    assert by_route["P_GLOBAL_RELATION"]["status"] == "OPEN_NO_OWNER"
    assert by_route["E_SCALE"]["status"] == "OPEN_NO_OWNER"
    assert by_route["E_SNE"]["status"] == "COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY"
    assert by_route["E_XMAX"]["status"] == "NECESSARY_REQUIREMENT_ONLY"
    assert by_route["S_GEOMETRY"]["status"] == "OPEN_NO_OWNER"
    assert by_route["S_MULTICHANNEL"]["status"] == "CONDITIONAL_IDENTIFIABILITY_ONLY"


def caught(result: dict, routes: list[dict[str, str]], mutation) -> bool:
    trial_result = copy.deepcopy(result)
    trial_routes = copy.deepcopy(routes)
    mutation(trial_result, trial_routes)
    try:
        validate(trial_result, trial_routes)
    except AssertionError:
        return True
    return False


def set_route(routes: list[dict[str, str]], route: str, status: str) -> None:
    next(row for row in routes if row["route"] == route)["status"] = status


def main() -> None:
    result = json.loads((HERE / "DERIVATION_RESULT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    routes = table(HERE / "OWNER_ROUTE_LEDGER.tsv")
    validate(result, routes)
    catches = {
        "missing_profile_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda r, _: r["family"].__setitem__("profiles", 590)),
        "sampled_to_continuum_promotion_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda r, _: r.__setitem__("maximum_scope", "continuum_global_theorem")),
        "regularity_profile_selection_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda _, q: set_route(q, "P_REGULARITY", "OWNED_NATIVE")),
        "global_relation_profile_selection_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda _, q: set_route(q, "P_GLOBAL_RELATION", "OWNED_NATIVE")),
        "scale_free_promotion_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda r, _: r["scale_factorization"].__setitem__("non_implication", "UDT_IS_SCALE_FREE")),
        "SNe_profile_copy_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda _, q: set_route(q, "E_SNE", "OWNED_NATIVE")),
        "Xmax_endpoint_identification_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda _, q: set_route(q, "E_XMAX", "OWNED_NATIVE")),
        "unrestricted_source_promotion_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda _, q: set_route(q, "S_GEOMETRY", "OWNED_NATIVE")),
        "conditional_identifiability_promotion_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda _, q: set_route(q, "S_MULTICHANNEL", "OWNED_NATIVE")),
        "native_owner_count_promotion_rejected": caught(result, routes, lambda r, _: r.__setitem__("owned_native_routes", 1)),
    }
    assert all(catches.values())
    output = {
        "schema": "udt-cmb-g78-catch-proofs-v1",
        "status": "PASS",
        "catch_count": len(catches),
        "catches": catches,
    }
    (HERE / "CATCH_PROOF_RESULTS.json").write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(output, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify G78 artifacts, preregistration, repository gates, and protected dirt."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path


HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent
BASE = "9a78af889321d84914ae5eb2c066da56bc957719"
PREREG = "bd500a09"
MANIFESTS = (
    "native_action_stage1_2026-07-18/arm_A/SHA256SUMS.txt",
    "native_action_stage1_2026-07-18/arm_B/SHA256SUMS.txt",
    "native_action_stage2_2026-07-18/arm_A/SHA256SUMS.txt",
    "native_action_stage2_2026-07-18/arm_B/SHA256SUMS.txt",
    "native_action_arm_c_2026-07-18/SHA256SUMS.txt",
    "native_action_final_adjudication_2026-07-18/SHA256SUMS.txt",
)
PROTECTED_PREFIX = "udt_native_onshell_timelive_reset_owner_audit_2026-08-10/"
PROTECTED_COUNT = 7


def table(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        return list(csv.DictReader(stream, delimiter="\t"))


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def run(command: list[str], timeout: int = 300) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(command, cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, check=False)


def main() -> None:
    assert run(["git", "merge-base", "--is-ancestor", PREREG, "HEAD"]).returncode == 0
    prereg_paths = run(["git", "diff-tree", "--no-commit-id", "--name-only", "-r", PREREG])
    assert set(prereg_paths.stdout.splitlines()) == {
        f"{HERE.name}/PREREGISTRATION.md", f"{HERE.name}/SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv"
    }

    manifest = table(HERE / "SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv")
    assert len(manifest) == len({row["path"] for row in manifest}) == 20
    for row in manifest:
        data = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{BASE}:{row['path']}"], cwd=ROOT)
        assert hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() == row["sha256"]

    expected_files = {
        "AUDIT_REPORT.md", "CATCH_PROOF_RESULTS.json", "DEPENDENCY_GRAPH.tsv",
        "DERIVATION_RESULT.json", "EXACT_DERIVATION.md", "INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION.json",
        "OWNER_ROUTE_LEDGER.tsv", "PREMISE_LEDGER.tsv", "PREREGISTRATION.md",
        "SOURCE_ADJUDICATION.tsv", "SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv", "derive_owner_join.py",
        "run_catch_proofs.py", "verify_owner_join_independent.py", "verify_package.py",
    }
    assert expected_files <= {path.name for path in HERE.iterdir() if path.is_file()}
    assert len(table(HERE / "OWNER_ROUTE_LEDGER.tsv")) == 7
    assert len(table(HERE / "SOURCE_ADJUDICATION.tsv")) == 20
    assert len(table(HERE / "PREMISE_LEDGER.tsv")) == 12

    result = json.loads((HERE / "DERIVATION_RESULT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    independent = json.loads((HERE / "INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    catches = json.loads((HERE / "CATCH_PROOF_RESULTS.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert result["status"] == independent["status"] == catches["status"] == "PASS"
    assert result["owned_native_routes"] == independent["owned_native_routes"] == 0
    assert result["family"]["profiles"] == independent["profile_rows"] == 591
    assert catches["catch_count"] == 10 and all(catches["catches"].values())

    report = (HERE / "AUDIT_REPORT.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    exact = (HERE / "EXACT_DERIVATION.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for token in (
        "PROVISIONAL_INTERNALLY_VERIFIED__FRESH_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED",
        "NO_PHYSICAL_PROFILE_ENDPOINT_SCALE_OR_SOURCE_OWNER_IN_FROZEN_G78_UNIVERSE",
        "This does **not** mean UDT is scale-free",
        "The control sphere `x=1` is not `X_max`",
        "COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY",
    ):
        assert token in report + exact

    premise = run(["python3", "verify_current_scientific_premises.py"], 60)
    assert premise.returncode == 0 and "PASS: 70 premise guards" in premise.stdout
    tests = run(["python3", "-m", "pytest", "-q", "tests/"], 300)
    assert tests.returncode == 0 and "98 passed, 1 xfailed" in tests.stdout

    frozen_members = 0
    for relative in MANIFESTS:
        manifest_path = ROOT / relative
        for line in manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            expected, member = line.split(None, 1)
            assert digest(manifest_path.parent / member.strip()) == expected
            frozen_members += 1
    assert frozen_members == 127

    current = table(ROOT / "research/_registry/CURRENT_ARTIFACT_PATHS.tsv")
    current_paths = [row["current_path"] for row in current]
    assert len(current_paths) == len(set(current_paths)) == 1114
    assert all((ROOT / path).exists() for path in current_paths)

    status = run(["git", "status", "--porcelain=v1", "--untracked-files=all"], 60)
    protected = []
    unexpected = []
    for line in status.stdout.splitlines():
        path = line[3:]
        if path.startswith(f"{HERE.name}/"):
            continue
        if line.startswith("?? ") and path.startswith(PROTECTED_PREFIX):
            protected.append(path)
            continue
        unexpected.append(line)
    assert len(protected) == PROTECTED_COUNT and not unexpected, unexpected

    output = {
        "schema": "udt-cmb-g78-package-verification-v1",
        "status": "PASS",
        "preregistration_commit": PREREG,
        "source_rows": len(manifest),
        "owner_routes": 7,
        "owned_native_routes": 0,
        "profile_rows": 591,

        "premise_guards": 70,

        "frozen_package_paths": frozen_members + len(MANIFESTS),
        "current_paths": len(current_paths),
        "protected_untracked_paths": len(protected),
        "protected_contents_read": False,
        "unexpected_dirty_paths": unexpected,
    }
    (HERE / "PACKAGE_VERIFICATION.json").write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(output, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# G78 preregistration — profile, endpoint/scale, and source-owner join

Date: 2026-08-11

Base: `9a78af889321d84914ae5eb2c066da56bc957719`

Question type: **METRIC-LED OWNERSHIP AUDIT**, not a CMB fit or profile-ranking exercise.

## Whole question

Does the current complete G65/G69--G77 evidence supply a native or observationally calibrated
owner for any of the three inputs still left open by the verified G77 relation family:

1. the physical intervening profile;
2. the physical comparison endpoint or global scale; or
3. the physical source state, including shape and normalization?

The test must distinguish a mathematical evaluator from a physical selector. A quantity supplied
as a query input, an atlas coordinate, a compatibility anchor, or an asymptotic requirement is not
an owner merely because it is useful.

## Frozen source universe

The exact twenty sources and their base-commit hashes are frozen in `SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv`. No
post-outcome source may enter this audit. The seven protected untracked stopped-draft files are
outside the source universe and must remain unread and untouched.

## Preregistered candidate routes

| route | candidate ownership mechanism | acceptance condition |
|---|---|---|
| `P_REGULARITY` | center smoothness, Lorentz regularity, and global sky topology | selects a proper subset of the 591 controls by a theory-owned physical condition, not a numerical pass/fail convention |
| `P_GLOBAL_RELATION` | complete relation-map topology or source-free response | supplies an intrinsic invariant with a declared rule that selects a profile/equivalence class |
| `E_SCALE` | `c_E`, symbolic `R`, or the control comparison sphere | supplies a derived physical scale or endpoint rather than a unit calibration/control coordinate |
| `E_SNE` | frozen P1 SNe relation | provides an owned map from the SNe pair relation into the G75 lapse/mixing profile and endpoint |
| `E_XMAX` | observer-pair positional-dilation asymptote | derives the current chart endpoint/scale and not merely a necessary limiting condition |
| `S_GEOMETRY` | metric response itself | derives source type, shape, normalization, or statistics rather than transporting supplied source data |
| `S_MULTICHANNEL` | area, shear, carry, and topology channels | removes unrestricted-source freedom without assuming a source law or physical observable projection |

## Exact algebraic and census tests

1. Reproduce the exact `591 = 49*4*3 + 3` G75 family census and the G77 `590/1/0`
   cross-method census.
2. Reproduce that every sampled G77 map has degree one, zero missing vertices, zero negative faces,
   zero negative projected maps, and no registered near-area defects.
3. Rewrite the G75 metric using `tau=c_E t/R` and verify exactly that
   `ds^2=R^2 dSigma^2`; classify what this does and does not remove from the null sky relation.
4. Check source congruence algebra independently for nonsymmetric invertible screen maps and
   positive-definite source covariances.
5. Type-check the SNe P1 variables against the G75 profile variables and require an explicit map
   before either can select the other.
6. Type-check `X_max` as an observer-pair asymptote against the chosen `x=1` comparison sphere and
   symbolic `R`; require an explicit realization map before identification.
7. Build a one-row-per-route ownership ledger and a dependency graph separating evaluator,
   compatibility anchor, necessary condition, control, and owner.

## Premise ledger

- `c_E`: `OBSERVED_FOUNDATIONAL_ANCHOR`; retained as clock/ruler calibration.
- `R>0`: `FREE_AND_UNSELECTED`; algebraically explored as an overall constant scale.
- G75 591-profile family: `CHOSE_BOUNDED_CONTROL`; complete only for its frozen quadratic rays.
- observer at `(1/4,0,0)`: `PINNED_BY_HISTORICAL_CONTROL`.
- comparison sphere `x=1`: `PINNED_BY_HISTORICAL_CONTROL`; not last scattering or `X_max`.
- G77 finite mesh and tolerances: `CHOSE_NUMERIC`; soundness controls only.
- SNe P1: `OBSERVED_CONDITIONAL_COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR`; not a CMB metric profile.
- `X_max`: `WORKING_FOUNDATIONAL_FRAME`; its value and realization remain open.
- source covariance/state: `FREE_AND_EXPLORED` in the algebraic positive-definite class; no source
  law is posited.
- bootstrap, action, matter, polarization, TT/TE/EE/BB population, and local signalling: `OPEN`,
  inactive, and forbidden as selectors here.

## Certification and falsification contract

The audit may return an owner only if the frozen sources contain the complete typed map and no
query input, control choice, observational fit, or asymptotic label is silently promoted. Otherwise
the route is `OPEN_NO_OWNER`, `COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY`, or `NECESSARY_REQUIREMENT_ONLY`.

Required catches must reject:

- dropping one of the 591 profiles because it is visually uninteresting;
- calling sampled finite-mesh regularity a continuum theorem;
- treating `R` cancellation from dimensionless rays as proof that UDT has no physical scale;
- identifying `x=1`, `R`, and `X_max` by name alone;
- copying P1 into the G75 lapse or mixing profile;
- treating an unrestricted source as metric-derived;
- calling conditional algebraic identifiability physical ownership; and
- ranking or fitting profiles against desired CMB features.

## Maximum conclusion

At most this audit can classify ownership within the exact frozen twenty-source universe and derive
the constant-scale covariance of the supplied stationary axial null relation. It cannot establish
a general UDT no-go, select a physical profile/source/endpoint, infer `X_max`, fit a spectrum,
derive polarization, activate bootstrap, or derive an action, matter source, or signalling law.

     1	# G78 exact derivation — profile, endpoint/scale, and source-owner join
     2	
     3	Date: 2026-08-11
     4	
     5	## 1. What is being joined
     6	
     7	G77 supplies a verified evaluator for 591 stationary axial control geometries under one supplied
     8	observer and comparison sphere. G78 asks whether any current result supplies the missing physical
     9	owner of the profile, endpoint/global scale, or source state. It does not ask which control looks
    10	most like the CMB.
    11	
    12	The frozen source universe has twenty individually adjudicated records. Seven candidate routes are
    13	typed in `OWNER_ROUTE_LEDGER.tsv`; none is silently enlarged after seeing the outcome.
    14	
    15	## 2. Regularity and topology do not select inside this family
    16	
    17	The exact G75 identity is
    18	
    19	```text
    20	591 = 49 primitive quadratic shapes * 4 amplitudes * 3 lapse controls + 3 zero controls.
    21	```
    22	
    23	G77 returns `590 STRONG_DIRECT_AGREEMENT`, one `REGISTERED_DIRECT_AGREEMENT`, and zero unresolved
    24	rows. All 591 maps have all 2,562 sampled directions crossing, sampled degree one, and zero missing,
    25	negative-face, negative-projected-map, or registered near-area defects.
    26	
    27	Therefore the registered center/signature/finite-mesh relation gates characterize all 591 controls
    28	as survivors. They select zero rows. This is not a continuum injectivity theorem and not a claim
    29	that regularity can never select in a broader family.
    30	
    31	## 3. Exact scale factorization
    32	
    33	The G75 metric is
    34	
    35	```text
    36	ds^2 = -A(x)c_E^2 dt^2 + R^2 dx^2/A(x)
    37	       + R^2 x^2(dtheta^2 + sin^2(theta)dpsi^2)
    38	       + 2 R c_E h(x) sin^2(theta) dt dpsi.
    39	```
    40	
    41	Define the dimensionless time coordinate
    42	
    43	```text
    44	tau = c_E t/R.
    45	```
    46	
    47	Then exactly
    48	
    49	```text
    50	ds^2 = R^2[-A dtau^2 + dx^2/A
    51	            + x^2(dtheta^2 + sin^2(theta)dpsi^2)
    52	            + 2 h sin^2(theta) dtau dpsi]
    53	     = R^2 dSigma^2.
    54	```
    55	
    56	Because `R` is constant, the Levi-Civita connection of `R^2 dSigma^2` in these dimensionless
    57	coordinates equals that of `dSigma^2`. Hence the unparameterized null paths and their dimensionless
    58	angular relation are independent of the numerical value of `R`. The same substitution uses `c_E`
    59	to define the clock/ruler-compatible time coordinate, after which `c_E` is absent from the
    60	dimensionless angular map.
    61	
    62	This does **not** mean UDT is scale-free. `c_E` remains the observed clock/ruler calibration and
    63	`R` remains the conversion from dimensionless `x` to physical length. The result is narrower:
    64	
    65	```text
    66	the G75--G77 dimensionless angular relation cannot determine its own absolute R.
    67	```
    68	
    69	An external distance/clock observable or a same-geometry cross-query relation is required.
    70	
    71	## 4. The control endpoint is not `X_max`
    72	
    73	G76/G77 choose the first outward crossing of `x=1`. The choice is part of the observer query. The
    74	current `X_max` frame instead requires a nonnegative observer-pair separation to approach a shared
    75	asymptote only as reciprocal depth diverges. It explicitly supplies no separation law, numerical
    76	value, chart endpoint, or identification with `R`.
    77	
    78	Thus `x=1`, `R`, and `X_max` have different types. No frozen source contains a map identifying
    79	them. The asymptotic requirement remains necessary for a future physical realization but is not an
    80	endpoint or scale owner here.
    81	
    82	## 5. The SNe anchor has the wrong current input type to select G75
    83	
    84	The frozen SNe P1 relation is
    85	
    86	```text
    87	r(phi_pair)=R_w[1-exp(-2 phi_pair/n)].
    88	```
    89	
    90	It is an observed conditional low-redshift observer-pair relation. G75 instead supplies a lapse
    91	control `A(x)=1+a x^2`, an axial mixing profile `h=x^2 q(x^2)`, and a chosen comparison sphere.
    92	No frozen source maps `(R_w,n,phi_pair)` into `(R,a,q,x_endpoint)` by deriving both from one complete
    93	geometry and query.
    94	
    95	P1 may later test a complete geometry for compatibility. Copying it into `A` or `q` would be a
    96	role error. It therefore remains `COMPATIBILITY_ANCHOR_ONLY`.
    97	
    98	## 6. Geometry transports source state but does not populate it
    99	
   100	For any invertible screen response `D` and positive-definite observed covariance `C_obs`, define
   101	
   102	```text
   103	C_src(D)=D^-1 C_obs D^-T.
   104	```
   105	
   106	Then exactly
   107	
   108	```text
   109	D C_src(D) D^T = C_obs,
   110	```
   111	
   112	and `C_src(D)` remains positive definite. G78 verifies the symbolic identity and an independent
   113	256-case nonsymmetric numerical reconstruction. Therefore an unrestricted source can absorb an
   114	invertible geometric response. The response evaluates and transports a source; it does not derive
   115	the source's shape, normalization, or statistics.
   116	
   117	G70's known-source-plus-carry model can distinguish controls conditionally. But the known source
   118	and observable carry channel are themselves unowned premises. Conditional identifiability is not
   119	physical ownership.
   120	
   121	This exact source statement applies on the invertible-response stratum. G77 supplies strong finite-
   122	mesh evidence that all 591 sampled relation maps are regular and orientation preserving, but G78
   123	does not promote that evidence to a continuum diffeomorphism theorem.
   124

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import numpy as np
rng = np.random.default_rng(7801)
max_relative = 0.0
min_source_eig = float('inf')
for _ in range(256):
    D = rng.normal(size=(2,2))
    while abs(np.linalg.det(D)) < 0.15:
        D = rng.normal(size=(2,2))
    B = rng.normal(size=(2,2))
    C_obs = B@B.T + 0.1*np.eye(2)
    C_src = np.linalg.inv(D) @ C_obs @ np.linalg.inv(D).T
    rec = D @ C_src @ D.T
    max_relative = max(max_relative, float(np.linalg.norm(rec-C_obs)/np.linalg.norm(C_obs)))
    min_source_eig = min(min_source_eig, float(np.linalg.eigvalsh(C_src).min()))
print('max_relative', format(max_relative, '.18e'))
print('min_source_eigenvalue', format(min_source_eig, '.18e'))

     1	# Xmax asymptotic-limit status and workflow
     2	
     3	Date: 2026-08-05
     4	Grade: owner-directed status correction; no new derivation
     5	
     6	## Controlling meaning
     7	
     8	`X_max` is the frame-shared positional-dilation asymptote for observer pairs. It is the separation
     9	limit approached as inter-frame dilation becomes infinite, structurally as `c` is the relative-speed
    10	limit approached at divergent Lorentz dilation in SR/GR.
    11	
    12	Writing `s(p,q)` only as the still-open nonnegative observer-pair separation type, and `delta` as
    13	the signed ordered comparison depth, the required realization has the limiting behavior
    14	
    15	```text
    16	0 <= s(p,q) < X_max                         for every finite comparison,
    17	s(p,q) -> X_max from below  => |delta| -> infinity,
    18	comparison dilation exp(|delta|) -> infinity.
    19	```
    20	
    21	This is a requirement on the missing physical realization. It is not a derivation of `s`, `delta(s)`,
    22	a tanh or fractional-linear formula, the value of `X_max`, or their angular/global completion.
    23	
    24	## Epistemic split
    25	
    26	- `WORKING_FOUNDATIONAL_FRAME`: the meaning of `X_max` as the positional-dilation asymptote.
    27	- `DERIVED`: reciprocal action on a supplied ordered depth.
    28	- `OPEN`: the general observer/event/path-to-depth and separation law.
    29	- `OPEN`: proof that the complete transformation law gives the same `X_max` to every admissible
    30	  observational frame.
    31	- `OPEN`: numerical `X_max`, local-WR-L/global join, angular modulation, bootstrap/density selection,
    32	  native mass relation, and complete global solution.
    33	
    34	The asymptote is relational. It is not a material wall, a preferred center, a radial edge, a
    35	finite-cell seal, or a terminal boundary of the manifold. A selected branch may represent the limit
    36	by a chart endpoint or regular causal horizon, but that identification must be derived separately.
    37	
    38	## Boundary consequence
    39	
    40	`X_max` alone supplies no boundary completion. It specifies a limiting behavior that a physical
    41	observer-pair law must realize. It does not specify what variables are varied, what data are fixed,
    42	which global completion is selected, or which surface term makes an action differentiable.
    43	
    44	Therefore future boundary work must first distinguish:
    45	
    46	1. the observer-pair asymptote;
    47	2. a chart endpoint representing it in a chosen solution;
    48	3. a causal horizon or observational-domain limit;
    49	4. an actual manifold boundary or seam; and
    50	5. the variational boundary data and functional.
    51	
    52	No two of these may be identified by name alone.
    53	
    54	## Required workflow gate
    55	
    56	Every proposed general depth/separation law must be evaluated against the whole complete-metric
    57	question before observational fitting or downstream action/matter work:
    58	
    59	1. Keep nonnegative separation distinct from signed orientation.
    60	2. Demonstrate the `X_max` asymptote without installing an ordinary wall or preferred center.
    61	3. Preserve neutral self-comparison and unchanged local physics.
    62	4. Demonstrate observer exchange/re-centering behavior and state whether the same limiting scale is
    63	   proven or only supplied.
    64	5. Retain angular, path, cut-locus, and global-completion data where load-bearing.
    65	6. Recover ordinary-regime calibration by observed `c_E` without importing SR/GR dynamics.
    66	7. Compare tanh, fractional-linear, WR-L, and other profiles only as candidates; do not choose them
    67	   from familiarity or endpoint behavior alone.
    68	8. Keep local WR-L `X` distinct from global `X_max` until the diameter/realization join is derived.
    69	9. Do not infer a numerical value, mass divergence, action, source, bootstrap law, boundary term, or
    70	   charge from the limit definition.
    71	
    72	Failure to realize the asymptote falsifies a candidate general UDT depth law. Passing the asymptote
    73	is necessary but not sufficient: many functions can share the same limiting behavior.
    74	
    75	## Effect on the current scientific frontier
    76	
    77	The current general-depth-law decision remains the right scientific seam, but its candidate space
    78	is now constrained. The next metric-led work should characterize observer-pair realization families
    79	that satisfy the asymptotic gate, rather than treating finite maximum separation as optional or
    80	searching for a physical outer wall. The stationary Killing law remains a branch-local anchor; the
    81	WR-L/SNe branch remains a conditional observational anchor.
    82	
    83	No old algebra is promoted by this correction. Historical statements that call the limiting role
    84	itself `OPEN` are superseded only on that semantic point; their open law, value, global-join,
    85	boundary, action, source, and mass conclusions remain controlling.

     1	# Exact native-query retyping of the frozen SNe profiles
     2	
     3	## 1. Correct object type
     4	
     5	The current pair-immersion architecture begins with a supplied regular calibrated relation
     6	`F:Sigma -> (M,g)` and its induced Lorentzian two-metric `h=F* g`. On the regular stratum,
     7	
     8	```text
     9	T^2=-h_00,
    10	L^2=h_11-h_01^2/h_00,
    11	phi_pair=(1/2)log(L/T)=(1/4)log[(-det h)/h_00^2].
    12	```
    13	
    14	This is a terminal evaluator. It does not select the SNe event pair, immersion, branch, screen, or
    15	flux transport.
    16	
    17	For the frozen SNe query, the registered observational identification is
    18	
    19	```text
    20	1+z=exp(phi_pair).
    21	```
    22	
    23	It follows conditionally that
    24	
    25	```text
    26	c_eff^(pair)/c_E=exp(-2 phi_pair)=(1+z)^(-2).
    27	```
    28	
    29	This is the supplied-pair cone readout. It is not a material propagation speed or a universal
    30	mixed-geometry law. `c_E` fixes the clock/ruler calibration but cancels from the dimensionless SNe
    31	shape.
    32	
    33	## 2. Frozen profile families in the corrected variable
    34	
    35	Writing `phi=phi_pair`, the three historical profile families are:
    36	
    37	```text
    38	P1: r(phi)=R_w [1-exp(-2 phi/n)],
    39	P2: r(phi)=2 X phi,
    40	P3: r(phi)=X [exp(2 phi/alpha)-1].
    41	```
    42	
    43	Substitution of `phi=log(1+z)` gives exactly:
    44	
    45	```text
    46	P1: r(z)=R_w [1-(1+z)^(-2/n)],
    47	P2: r(z)=2 X log(1+z),
    48	P3: r(z)=X [(1+z)^(2/alpha)-1].
    49	```
    50	
    51	Therefore the corrected terminal variable does not alter any historical profile formula.
    52	
    53	## 3. Registered area/flux readout
    54	
    55	The frozen SNe assembly additionally supplies
    56	
    57	```text
    58	d_A=r,
    59	d_L=(1+z)^2 d_A=exp(2 phi_pair) r(phi_pair).
    60	```
    61	
    62	Thus
    63	
    64	```text
    65	P1: d_L=(1+z)^2 R_w [1-(1+z)^(-2/n)],
    66	P2: d_L=2 X (1+z)^2 log(1+z),
    67	P3: d_L=X (1+z)^2 [(1+z)^(2/alpha)-1].
    68	```
    69	
    70	At `n=1`, P1 still reduces exactly to
    71	
    72	```text
    73	d_L=R_w z(z+2).
    74	```
    75	
    76	The exact checks are in `derive_query_equivalence.py` and `QUERY_EQUIVALENCE.json`.
    77	
    78	These area/flux relations are retained **conditional readout premises**. The pair metric alone does
    79	not derive its immersion, second fundamental form, full screen-area map, source physics, or flux
    80	transport.
    81	
    82	## 4. What the orchestra changes—and what it does not yet calculate
    83	
    84	The complete metric can contribute reciprocal, angular, shift, and mixing terms to the induced
    85	pair metric before `phi_pair` is evaluated. Therefore a complete SNe realization may change both:
    86	
    87	```text
    88	the map from the observer query to phi_pair,
    89	the map from that realization to d_A.
    90	```
    91	
    92	The current bank establishes this structural possibility. It does not select the physical SNe
    93	immersion, screen, branch, time-live history, or coefficient-free curve through the orchestra
    94	atlas. Consequently there is no owned additive “orchestra correction” to insert into P1/P2/P3.
    95	
    96	Retyping the already-fitted scalar as `phi_pair` is mathematically consistent, but it does not prove
    97	that the frozen P1 profile is the output of the complete native geometry. It changes the
    98	interpretation, not the fitted numbers.
    99	
   100	## 5. Exact scoped landing

    65	## 3. Exact source-covariance degeneracy
    66	
    67	Let an invertible geometric screen transfer be `D`, a positive-definite source covariance be
    68	`C_src`, and its transported covariance be
    69	
    70	```text
    71	C_obs = D C_src D^T.
    72	```
    73	
    74	For any positive-definite target `C_obs`, define
    75	
    76	```text
    77	C_src(D) = D^-1 C_obs D^-T.
    78	```
    79	
    80	Then directly
    81	
    82	```text
    83	D C_src(D) D^T = C_obs.
    84	```
    85	
    86	For every nonzero vector `v`,
    87	
    88	```text
    89	v^T C_src(D) v = (D^-T v)^T C_obs (D^-T v) > 0,
    90	```
    91	
    92	so the constructed source covariance remains positive definite. Therefore an unrestricted source
    93	covariance can exactly absorb any invertible geometric map. This is a congruence identity, not a
    94	numerical coincidence.
    95	
    96	All 315 G69 maps are invertible; their minimum singular value is `0.0496135`. Across the three
    97	registered covariance controls and all `945` reconstructions, the maximum relative backward error
    98	is `2.8306e-16` and the minimum constructed source eigenvalue is `1.56159`.
    99	
   100	## 4. Channel ownership consequence
   101	
   102	The geometric rank uses area, anisotropy, **and** azimuthal carry. A scalar TT readout does not
   103	directly read arbitrary screen orientation/carry. Projecting from three readout channels to two or
   104	one can only reduce rank. More importantly, even a complete local covariance readout cannot select
   105	`D` while `C_src` is unrestricted, by the exact identity above.
   106	
   107	Consequently:
   108	
   109	- the metric map contains genuine profile and endpoint information;
   110	- that information does not by itself identify the physical profile or endpoint from a sky
   111	  covariance;
   112	- a typed source/state restriction, an independently owned endpoint/profile rule, or genuinely
   113	  independent observational channels are required before coefficients can be estimated;
   114	- an observational fit cannot be used to pretend that an unrestricted source has been derived.

     2

     7	`METRIC_OWNS_SOURCE_FREE_SCREEN_RESPONSE__PHYSICAL_OBSERVABLE_OPEN`

    10	not a physical CMB query, source, spectrum, polarization law, or prediction.
    11	
    12	## 1. Correctly typed screen data
    13	
    14	Let `S_s` and `S_o` be oriented positive-definite two-dimensional screen spaces at the source and
    15	observer ends of one supplied path/query. The complete metric conditionally owns:
    16	
    17	```text
    18	D:S_s -> S_o       finite Jacobi/image response,
    19	U:S_s -> S_o       metric screen isometry along the same supplied path.
    20	```
    21	
    22	Strictly, a null Jacobi map sends an initial transverse derivative/angle into endpoint separation.
    23	The comparison with `U` is therefore well typed only after the query supplies the source-screen
    24	metric, affine calibration, and identification used in the Jacobi initial condition. G68 does this
    25	by setting `P_B(0)=E_B` in its registered affine parameter. A positive rescaling of that affine
    26	parameter changes the common response scale but not shear ratio or polar angle.
    27	
    28	The result does not authorize combining a Jacobi map from one query with normal transport from a
    29	different pair immersion or path.
    30	
    31	## 2. Endpoint gauges and why one open transport has no angle
    32	
    33	In oriented orthonormal endpoint frames, let independent frame changes be
    34	
    35	```text
    36	R_s in SO(2),  R_o in SO(2).
    37	```
    38	
    39	Then
    40	
    41	```text
    42	D -> D' = R_o D R_s^-1,
    43	U -> U' = R_o U R_s^-1.                                      (1)
    44	```
    45	
    46	The displayed rotation angle of `U` changes by the independent source/observer frame angles. The
    47	left-right action is transitive on `SO(2)`, so no order-zero real angular scalar belongs to `U`
    48	alone on one unframed open path. This reproduces the banked open-path gauge result.
    49	
    50	## 3. The relative response is a new, correctly typed object
    51	
    52	Because `D` and `U` share both endpoints, define
    53	
    54	```text
    55	M = U^-1 D:S_s -> S_s.                                      (2)
    56	```
    57	
    58	Equation (1) gives
    59	
    60	```text
    61	M -> M' = R_s M R_s^-1.                                    (3)
    62	```
    63	
    64	The observer-end gauge has cancelled. The remaining source gauge acts by conjugation, not by
    65	independent left and right multiplication. That change of type is what permits relative angular
    66	information to survive.
    67	
    68	This is not a preferred path or physical query selector. It is the response assigned after the
    69	path/query has already been supplied.

     3	Date: 2026-08-11
     4	
     5	This additions-only layer narrows one sentence-level ambiguity found by sealed external review. It
     6	does not change the G73 source-recovery algebra, strong-shear limit, G68 measurements, or ownership

     9	## Correct general statement
    10	
    11	A robust repeated-image kaleidoscope requires a global noninjective/branching relation, a singular
    12	or critical map in the whole-sky `S^2` self-map case, or restrictions on the source family.
    13	
    14	Singularity is not a universal requirement for every repeated-image geometry.
    15	
    16	## Exact whole-sky theorem
    17	
    18	Let `f:S^2 -> S^2` be smooth and everywhere a local diffeomorphism. Since the source is compact,
    19	`f` is proper, hence a covering map. The target `S^2` is connected and simply connected, so every
    20	connected covering is one-sheeted. Therefore `f` is a diffeomorphism and cannot supply a nontrivial
    21	repeated whole-sky self-image. A nontrivial repeated `S^2` self-image must leave the everywhere-
    22	regular class through critical/branch points.
    23	
    24	## Cases outside that theorem
    25	
    26	- With different source/target topology, a regular covering of degree greater than one can exist.
    27	- On a partial-sky or noncompact domain, the compact connected `S^2` covering argument does not
    28	  apply; a regular noninjective map may exist.
    29	- A branch-labelled relation is not necessarily one single-valued map. Several regular paths may
    30	  coexist without a caustic, while their selection or combination remains unowned.
    31	- Strong shear can dominate normalized direction while remaining invertible and producing no
    32	  repeated image.
    33	
    34	No current UDT source selects any one of these global cases. The next gate remains a complete
    35	symbolic-scale sky-relation topology/critical atlas.

    21	def rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    22	    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
    23	        return list(csv.DictReader(stream, delimiter="\t"))
    24

    26	def digest_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    27	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    28	
    29	
    30	def verify_sources() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    31	    manifest = rows(HERE / "SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv")
    32	    assert len(manifest) == 20
    33	    assert len({row["path"] for row in manifest}) == 20
    34	    for row in manifest:
    35	        assert row["source_commit"] == BASE
    36	        data = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{BASE}:{row['path']}"], cwd=ROOT)
    37	        assert digest_bytes(data) == row["sha256"], row["path"]
    38	    return manifest
    39	
    40	
    41	def exact_scale_factorization() -> dict[str, object]:
    42	    R, c_e, A, h, st = sp.symbols("R c_E A h sin_theta", positive=True, nonzero=True)
    43	    dt_dtau = R / c_e
    44	    original = {
    45	        "tau_tau": -A * c_e**2 * dt_dtau**2,
    46	        "x_x": R**2 / A,
    47	        "theta_theta": R**2,
    48	        "psi_psi_unit_x2": R**2,
    49	        "tau_psi": 2 * R * c_e * h * st**2 * dt_dtau,
    50	    }
    51	    expected = {
    52	        "tau_tau": -A,
    53	        "x_x": 1 / A,
    54	        "theta_theta": 1,
    55	        "psi_psi_unit_x2": 1,
    56	        "tau_psi": 2 * h * st**2,
    57	    }
    58	    checks = {key: sp.simplify(original[key] / R**2 - expected[key]) == 0 for key in original}
    59	    assert all(checks.values())
    60	    return {
    61	        "substitution": "tau=c_E*t/R",
    62	        "factorization": "ds^2=R^2*dSigma^2",
    63	        "coefficient_checks": checks,
    64	        "interpretation": "dimensionless_null_sky_relation_independent_of_constant_R_and_unit_calibration_cE",
    65	        "non_implication": "does_not_make_UDT_scale_free_or_select_R_endpoint_or_Xmax",
    66	    }
    67	
    68	
    69	def exact_source_congruence() -> dict[str, object]:
    70	    a, b, c, d, u, v, w = sp.symbols("a b c d u v w")
    71	    D = sp.Matrix([[a, b], [c, d]])
    72	    C_obs = sp.Matrix([[u, v], [v, w]])
    73	    C_src = D.inv() * C_obs * D.inv().T
    74	    residual = sp.simplify(D * C_src * D.T - C_obs)
    75	    assert residual == sp.zeros(2)
    76	
    77	    max_relative = 0.0
    78	    minimum_source_eigenvalue = float("inf")
    79	    controls = 0
    80	    for k in range(1, 17):
    81	        Dn = np.array([[1.0 + k / 19.0, (-1) ** k / 7.0], [k / 23.0, 0.8 + k / 31.0]])
    82	        if abs(np.linalg.det(Dn)) < 0.1:
    83	            continue
    84	        B = np.array([[1.0 + k / 13.0, k / 29.0], [(-1) ** k / 11.0, 0.7 + k / 17.0]])
    85	        Cn = B @ B.T + 0.2 * np.eye(2)
    86	        source = np.linalg.solve(Dn, Cn) @ np.linalg.inv(Dn).T
    87	        reconstructed = Dn @ source @ Dn.T
    88	        relative = np.linalg.norm(reconstructed - Cn) / np.linalg.norm(Cn)
    89	        max_relative = max(max_relative, float(relative))
    90	        minimum_source_eigenvalue = min(minimum_source_eigenvalue, float(np.linalg.eigvalsh(source).min()))
    91	        controls += 1
    92	    assert controls == 16 and max_relative < 2e-15 and minimum_source_eigenvalue > 0
    93	    return {
    94	        "symbolic_residual_zero": True,
    95	        "deterministic_controls": controls,
    96	        "maximum_relative_residual": max_relative,
    97	        "minimum_source_eigenvalue": minimum_source_eigenvalue,
    98	        "interpretation": "invertible_geometry_transports_arbitrary_positive_definite_source_data_but_does_not_derive_it",
    99	    }
   100	
   101	
   102	def family_census() -> dict[str, object]:
   103	    profiles = rows(ROOT / "udt_cmb_G75_center_regular_axial_profile_family_2026-08-11/PROFILE_ATLAS.tsv")
   104	    direct = rows(ROOT / "udt_cmb_G77_full_family_direct_christoffel_replay_2026-08-11/DIRECT_CHRISTOFFEL_ATLAS.tsv")
   105	    assert len(profiles) == len(direct) == 591
   106	    assert len({row["profile_id"] for row in profiles}) == 591
   107	    assert {row["profile_id"] for row in profiles} == {row["profile_id"] for row in direct}
   108	    nonzero = [row for row in profiles if row["shape_id"] != "ZERO"]
   109	    zero = [row for row in profiles if row["shape_id"] == "ZERO"]
   110	    assert len(nonzero) == 49 * 4 * 3 == 588 and len(zero) == 3
   111	    classes: dict[str, int] = {}
   112	    for row in direct:
   113	        classes[row["direct_class"]] = classes.get(row["direct_class"], 0) + 1
   114	        assert int(row["crossed_vertices"]) == 2562
   115	        assert int(row["missing_vertices"]) == 0
   116	        assert int(row["negative_faces"]) == 0
   117	        assert int(row["negative_projected_face_maps"]) == 0
   118	        assert int(row["near_area_1e2"]) == 0
   119	        assert abs(float(row["degree"]) - 1.0) < 5e-15
   120	    assert classes == {"STRONG_DIRECT_AGREEMENT": 590, "REGISTERED_DIRECT_AGREEMENT": 1}
   121	    return {

   160	def write_tsv(path: Path, data: list[dict[str, str]]) -> None:
   161	    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
   162	        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=list(data[0]), delimiter="\t", lineterminator="\n")
   163	        writer.writeheader()
   164	        writer.writerows(data)

   180	        "source_rows": len(manifest),

   183	        "source_congruence": source,

   185	        "owned_native_routes": sum(row["status"] == "OWNED_NATIVE" for row in routes),
   186	        "next_gate": "derive_or_supply_one_complete_physical_query_global_completion_or_native_source_state_map_before_any_fit",
   187	        "maximum_scope": "exact_twenty_source_ownership_audit_plus_frozen_591_profile_finite_mesh_census",

    33	ray,
    34	
    35	```text
    36	M_p = max_{0<=s<=1}|p(s)|,
    37	q(s)=epsilon p(s)/M_p,
    38	epsilon in {1/20,1/5,1/2,1},
    39	a in {-1/4,0,1/4}.
    40	```
    41	
    42	For a quadratic, the exact normalization candidates are `s=0`, `s=1`, and the vertex
    43	`s=-c1/(2c2)` when it lies strictly between them. Thus every `M_p` is rational and no floating root
    44	or optimizer enters. The 49 rays, four amplitudes, and three lapse controls give 588 nonzero-mixing
    45	profiles. Adding the three matched `q=0` controls gives 591.

**Caveats**

The surviving landing is bounded to the frozen 20-source universe plus the stationary-axial finite-mesh G75-G77 family. It is not a continuum injectivity theorem, not a generic UDT no-go, and not a proof that no future same-geometry cross-query can own endpoint/scale or source selection.

**Smallest next calculation**

Derive one same-geometry dimensional SNe query on the already-fixed G75/G77 control/query, returning `phi_pair` and `d_A` or `d_L` for a single realization, before any P1 comparison or family fit. That is smaller than a full cross-query atlas and is the first calculation that could directly pressure the open endpoint/scale route.
